# Document-collaboration ingress inventory (CC-WP01)

This is normative inventory data for routing live document transaction, history, and recompute ingress. It deliberately makes **no** schema-44 authorization or terminal-verdict design decision; that decision belongs to the ADR.

## Classification legend

- **migrate** — a direct legacy ingress that must enter the coordinator-owned route.
- **compatibility delegation** — a public/legacy façade retained temporarily, delegating to the target route.
- **typed adapter required** — an externally callable or multi-document operation whose request shape and atomic scope must be explicit; it must not be treated as an implicit compatibility exemption.
- **bootstrap/restore exception** — a narrowly bounded load, rollback-stabilization, or recovery path; it remains direct only for that lifecycle purpose.
- **non-ingress definition/helper** — primitive definition or internal helper that establishes the boundary; it is not an independently callable ingress.

`current owner` identifies the component presently making/controlling the primitive; `target owner/route` identifies the intended routing owner, not an implementation decision. “Coordinator” means the document-scoped `App::DocumentCommitCoordinator` route. `CC-WP02` through `CC-WP12` are the only migration-WP values used below.

## Coverage method and completion rule

Search roots are `src/App`, `src/Gui`, `src/Mod`, `src/Tools`, `tools/mcp/freecad-mcp/src/freecad_mcp`, and `tools/mcp/freecad-mcp/addon/FreeCADMCP`. The audit command is deliberately source-root scoped; `src/Mod` is not excluded merely because it contains test/example children. Excluded path components are exactly `test`, `tests`, `Test*`, `*Tests`, `femtest`, `femexamples`, `drafttests`, `parttests`, `bimtests`, `CAMTests`, `TDTest`, `SketcherTests`, `AssemblyTests`, `examples`, `Example*`, `benchmarks`, `doc`, `docs`, and `Resources`; excluded file extensions are `.ts`, `.json`, `.txt`, `.md`, `.dox`, `.ui`, and `.pyi`. This excludes generated data/snapshots and translations, but not callable generated Python wrappers.

The concrete reproduction command is:

```powershell
$roots = @('src/App','src/Gui','src/Mod','src/Tools','tools/mcp/freecad-mcp/src/freecad_mcp','tools/mcp/freecad-mcp/addon/FreeCADMCP')
$pat = '(openTransaction\s*\(|commitTransaction\s*\(|abortTransaction\s*\(|setActiveTransaction\s*\(|setUndo\w*\s*\(|clearUndo\w*\s*\(|clearRedo\w*\s*\(|\.(undo|redo|recompute|recomputeFeature|recomputeObjects|partialRecompute)\s*\(|->(undo|redo|recompute|recomputeFeature|recomputeObjects|partialRecompute)\s*\(|AutoTransaction\s*\(|(Gui::)?doCommand\s*\(|FCMD_DOC_CMD\s*\()'
$excluded = '(^|[\\/])(tests?|Test[^\\/]*|[^\\/]*Tests|femtest|femexamples|drafttests|parttests|bimtests|CAMTests|TDTest|SketcherTests|AssemblyTests|examples?|benchmarks|doc|docs|Resources|typing[\\/]smoke)([\\/]|$)'
$badExtension = '\.(ts|json|txt|md|dox|ui|pyi)$'
$raw = rg -n --no-heading $pat $roots
$records = foreach ($entry in $raw) {
    if ($entry -match '^(?<path>.*?):(?<line>\d+):(?<text>.*)$') {
        [pscustomobject]@{ Path=$Matches.path; Line=[int]$Matches.line; Text=$Matches.text }
    }
}
$filtered = $records | Where-Object {
    $_.Path -notmatch $excluded -and $_.Path -notmatch $badExtension -and $_.Text -notmatch '^\s*(//|#|\*)'
}
$raw.Count; $filtered.Count; ($filtered.Path | Sort-Object -Unique).Count
$filtered | Sort-Object Path,Line | ForEach-Object { '{0}:{1}:{2}' -f $_.Path,$_.Line,$_.Text }
```

Searched families are `openTransaction`, `commitTransaction`, `abortTransaction`, `setActiveTransaction`, direct `undo`, `redo`, and direct full/partial `recompute`, plus command-string forms. C++, Python, `Gui::Command::doCommand`, `Gui.doCommand`, `FCMD_DOC_CMD`, RAII wrappers, Python bindings, macros, and generated executable Python are calls when they can reach a live `App::Document`.

### Audit line-to-symbol association

Every corrected-command occurrence is associated with one exact source symbol. Python uses the enclosing `def`/`async def` (qualified by class); C++ uses the enclosing callable; declarations and non-executed documentation use their exact declared symbol or owning source symbol. The main and reviewed-exclusion tables form a disjoint occurrence partition.

Corrected-command measurement: 5,285 raw occurrences; 3,376 filtered candidates in 541 union files. Retained actual ingress: 3,059 occurrences in 516 files and 1365 main-table rows (including one explicit generic Python bridge and the two explicit lifecycle exceptions outside the textual occurrence partition). Reviewed non-ingress: 317 occurrences in 94 files and 245 exclusion rows. Retained plus reviewed-exclusion occurrences equal 3,376 exactly; 69 files contain both kinds, so file counts are not added. Zero corrected-command occurrences remain unclassified.

Unsupported cross-document atomic operations remain `typed adapter required` (or are rejected); they are never a broad exception.

Known limits are dynamic Python content supplied by users at runtime, third-party add-ons outside the listed roots, and reflection/function pointers that no textual scan can resolve. Those are covered at their callable bridge (binding, macro runner, GUI command, or typed MCP adapter), without inventing a source location.

## Summary mapping

- `CC-WP02`: foundation/static gate only.
- `CC-WP03`: core App, `DocumentPy`, undo/redo, and lifecycle/recovery/save-related model mutation controls.
- `CC-WP04`: `Gui::Command`, `Gui::Document`, Property Editor, common task panels, and GUI-facing Python bridges.
- `CC-WP05`: remaining module/workbench ingress.
- `CC-WP10`: recompute-coordinator foundation.
- `CC-WP11`: generic isolated feature recompute.
- `CC-WP12`: async/sync recompute façade, internal GUI recompute migration, and MCP typed recompute ingress.

Measured main-inventory rows: 1,365 across 518 source files. Classification counts: bootstrap/restore exception 2; migrate 1309; typed adapter required 54. WP counts: CC-WP03 28; CC-WP04 364; CC-WP05 911; CC-WP11 4; CC-WP12 58. Domain counts: App Python 9; App core 23; Assembly task panel 1; Assembly workbench 41; BIM workbench 211; CAM workbench 131; Draft workbench 109; Fem task panel 49; Fem workbench 83; GUI Python 1; GUI core 91; GUI property editor 5; GUI tooling 2; Import workbench 8; Inspection workbench 1; MCP addon 36; MCP typed ingress 5; Measure task panel 4; Mesh workbench 28; MeshPart workbench 7; OpenSCAD workbench 18; Part task panel 27; Part workbench 96; PartDesign task panel 76; PartDesign workbench 33; Points workbench 5; ReverseEngineering workbench 9; Robot task panel 3; Robot workbench 12; Sketcher task panel 11; Sketcher workbench 25; Spreadsheet workbench 33; Start workbench 1; Surface task panel 40; Surface workbench 6; TechDraw task panel 71; TechDraw workbench 44; TemplatePyMod workbench 10.
